\displaystyle\leq C(\iota,p,T),

where one has used the fact that
2-3\alpha+2\iota-2\varepsilon>-\alpha
.

To handle
\mathcal{J}_{32}
, one can obtain from the same calculations of
\mathcal{J}_{2}
that

(6.25)

\mathcal{J}_{32}\leq C(\iota,p,T)+\frac{1}{8}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{\left|U\right|^{\frac{p}{2}}U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Substituting (
6.21
), (
6.24
)-(
6.25
) into (
6.23
) yields

(6.26)

\mathcal{J}_{3}\leq C(p)|\mathcal{J}_{1}|+C(\iota,p,T)+\frac{1}{8}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{\left|U\right|^{\frac{p}{2}}U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(\iota,p,T)+\frac{1}{8}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{\left|U\right|^{\frac{p}{2}}U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Finally, we treat
\mathcal{J}_{4}
. For any fixed
\varepsilon\in(0,1)
, it follows from Lemmas
6.4
,
6.5
and
6.7
, (
6.1
) and Hölder’s inequality that

(6.27)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{4}

\displaystyle=-2\int\rho_{0}^{1+\iota}H_{x}\left|U\right|^{p}U\,\mathrm{d}x=-2\int\rho_{0}^{2+\iota}(V-U)\left|U\right|^{p}U\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq 2\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{2+\iota}{p+2}}U\big|_{p+2}^{p+2}+2\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha}{2}+\varepsilon}V\big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{4+2\iota-\alpha-2\varepsilon}{2p+2}}U\big|_{2p+2}^{p+1}\leq C(\iota,p,T),

which, along with (
6.20
)-(
6.22
), (
6.26
)-(
6.27
) and Grönwall’s inequality, yields the desired estimates.

The proof of Lemma
6.9
is completed.
∎

The following auxiliary lemma relates to the weighted
L^{2}
-estimates of
U_{x}
.

Lemma 6.10
.

For any
T>0
,
0<\alpha\leq 1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle+\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\iota,T),

for all
0\leq t\leq T
, where
\iota=0
if
0<\alpha<1
and
\iota>0
if
\alpha=1
.

Proof.

Step 1: Tangential estimates.

Let
\iota=0
if
0<\alpha<1
and
\iota>0
if
\alpha=1
. Multiplying
\eqref{secondreformulation}_{1}
by
\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+\iota}\eta_{x}U_{t}
and integrating the resulting equality over
I
, one gets

(6.28)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}}\,\mathrm{d}x+\int\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}\eta_{x}U_{t}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\frac{1}{2}\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}U_{x}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x-2\int\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}H_{x}U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\underline{-\frac{2\alpha+\iota}{\alpha}\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{\alpha+1+\iota}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{x}U_{t}}{\eta_{x}}\,\mathrm{d}x-\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}\eta_{xx}U_{x}U_{t}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x}_{:=\mathcal{J}_{7}}:=\sum_{i=5}^{7}\mathcal{J}_{i}.

For
\mathcal{J}_{5}
, one can deduce from integration by parts,
U_{x}|_{x\in\Gamma}=0
, Lemma
6.4
, Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality that for all
0<\varepsilon<1
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{5}

\displaystyle=-\frac{1}{2}\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}U_{x}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=-\left.\frac{1}{2}\frac{\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}UU_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\right|_{x=0}^{x=1}+\frac{2\alpha+1+\iota}{2\alpha}\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{\alpha+1+\iota}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}UU_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\quad+\int\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}\frac{UU_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x

(6.29)

\displaystyle=\frac{2\alpha+1+\iota}{2\alpha}\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{\alpha+1+\iota}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}UU_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x+\int\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}\frac{UU_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C(\iota,T)\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{UU_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C(\iota)\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{UU_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\iota,\varepsilon,T)\sum_{j=0}^{1}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{\left|U\right|^{j}U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}+\varepsilon\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}^{2}.

For
\mathcal{J}_{6}
, it follows from (
6.1
), Lemmas
6.2
-
6.4
,
6.5
and
6.7
, Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality that

(6.30)

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{6}

\displaystyle=-2\int\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}H_{x}U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}x=-2\int\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+2+\iota}(V-U)U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq 2\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{3+\iota}{2}}V\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{3+\iota}{2}}U\big|_{2}\big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\iota,T)+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

At last, for
\mathcal{J}_{7}
, it follows from (
6.1
), Lemmas
6.4
and
6.6
, Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality that

\displaystyle\mathcal{J}_{7}

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\alpha+\iota}{\alpha}\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{\alpha+1+\iota}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{x}U_{t}}{\eta_{x}}\,\mathrm{d}x-\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}\eta_{xx}U_{x}U_{t}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=-\frac{2\alpha+1+\iota}{\alpha}\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{\alpha+1+\iota}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{x}U_{t}}{\eta_{x}}\,\mathrm{d}x+\int\rho_{0}^{2\alpha+1+\iota}(V-U)U_{x}U_{t}\,\mathrm{d}x

(6.31)

\displaystyle\leq C(\iota,T)\left(\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}\right)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C(\iota)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{UU_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\iota,T)\sum_{j=0}^{1}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{\left|U\right|^{j}U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

Thus, it follows from (
6.28
)-(
6.31
) that for all
0<\varepsilon<1
,

(6.32)

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle C(\iota,\varepsilon,T)\Big(1+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{\left|U\right|^{j}U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big)+\varepsilon\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Step 2: Elliptic estimates.

For the elliptic term, one can multiply both sides of
\eqref{secondreformulation}_{1}
by
\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{\iota-1}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}
and apply (
6.1
) to the resulting equality to get that

\displaystyle\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}=\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}-\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}(V-U)\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}+2\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{3+\iota}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}(V-U).

Then taking the
L^{2}
-norm of both sides of the above equality, one gets from Lemmas
6.4
and
6.6
-
6.7
that

(6.33)

\displaystyle\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}\big|_{2}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{{\eta_{x}}}}\Big|_{2}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{UU_{x}}{\sqrt{{\eta_{x}}}}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C(T)\big(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{3+\iota}{2\alpha}}\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{3+\iota}{2}}U\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}\big|_{2}+C(T)\sum_{j=0}^{1}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{\left|U\right|^{j}U_{x}}{\sqrt{{\eta_{x}}}}\Big|_{2}+C(\iota,T).

Plugging (
6.33
) into (
6.32
) and then choosing
\varepsilon
sufficiently small, one gets that

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(\iota,T)\Big(1+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{\left|U\right|^{j}U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}\Big).

Integrating above over
[0,t]
, one can obtain from Lemma
6.9
that

(6.34)

\displaystyle\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}(t)\Big|_{2}^{2}\!+\!\!\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\sqrt{\eta_{x}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\mathrm{d}s\!\leq\!C(\iota,T)\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}(u_{0})_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}+1\big)\!\leq\!C(\iota,T),

where one has used the fact that

\big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}(u_{0})_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}\leq\left|\rho_{0}\right|_{\infty}^{2\alpha+\iota}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(u_{0})_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(\iota)\widetilde{E}(0,U)\leq C(\iota).

Finally, it follows from (
6.33
)-(
6.34
) and Lemma
6.9
that for all
0\leq t\leq T
,

\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\iota,T).

The proof of Lemma
6.10
is completed.
∎

Consequently, the following corollary holds.

Corollary 6.2
.

For any
T>0
and
0<\alpha\leq 1
, it holds that

\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\iota,T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T,

where
\iota=0
if
0<\alpha<1
and
\iota>0
if
\alpha=1
.

Proof.

Let
\iota=0
if
0<\alpha<1
and
\iota>0
if
\alpha=1
. According to Lemmas
6.4
and
A.4
,
U_{x}|_{x\in\Gamma}=0
and Hölder’s inequality, one has

\displaystyle\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{\infty}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int\Big|\left(\rho_{0}^{\alpha+1+\iota}\frac{U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\right)_{x}\Big|\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq 2\int\rho_{0}^{\alpha+1+\iota}\Big|\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|\Big|\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|\,\mathrm{d}x+C(\iota)\int\rho_{0}^{1+\iota}\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|\frac{U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq 2\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}+C(\iota,T)\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big|\rho_{0}^{\alpha+\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{1}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}^{2}+C(\iota,T)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\sqrt{\eta_{x}}}\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Thus, integrating above over
[0,t]
and using Lemmas
6.9
and
6.10
lead to the desired conclusion.

The proof of Corollary
6.2
is completed.
∎

Now, one can establish the upper bound of
\eta_{x}
.

Lemma 6.11
.

For every
T>0
, it holds that

(6.35)

\sup_{(t,x)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}}\eta_{x}(t,x)\leq C(T).

Proof.

Step 1.
We first establish the upper bound of
\eta_{x}
in the interior of
I
. Since
\eta_{tx}=U_{x}
and
\eta_{x}(0,x)=1
, we have

\log\eta_{x}(t,x)=\int_{0}^{t}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}(s,x)\,\mathrm{d}s.

Then the Hölder inequality, together with Corollary
6.2
, implies

(6.36)

\rho_{0}^{\kappa}|\log\eta_{x}(t,x)|\leq t^{1/2}\Bigl(\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\kappa}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}(s)\Big|_{\infty}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Bigr)^{\frac{1}{2}}\leq C(T)

for all
(t,x)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}
, where

\kappa=\begin{cases}(\alpha+1)/2&\text{if }0<\alpha<1,\\
3/2&\text{if }\alpha=1.\end{cases}

Since
\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\sim d(x)
, it follows from (
6.36
) and Lemma
6.4
that, for every fixed
b\in(0,\frac{1}{2})
,

(6.37)

\eta_{x}(t,x)\leq C(b,T)\qquad\text{for }(t,x)\in[0,T]\times[b,1-b].

Step 2.
For the uniform upper bound of
\eta_{x}
near the vacuum boundary, repeating the same calculation (
6.11
)-(
6.13
) in Lemma
6.4
, we can obtain that, for any
q\geq 2
,

(6.38)

|F(t,x)|\leq C(q,T)\rho_{0}(x)^{(1+\alpha)(1-\frac{1}{q})}\qquad\text{for all $(t,x)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}$}.

Here,
F(t,x)
is defined as in Lemma
6.4
.

Next, it follows from Lemma
6.2
and equation (
6.10
) that

H_{t}+CH\geq H_{t}+H^{2}=-F_{t},

where the constant
C
is given as in Lemma
6.2
. Solving the above inequality, we deduce from
H(0,x)=\rho_{0}(x)
that

H(t,x)\geq e^{-Ct}\rho_{0}(x)-\int_{0}^{t}e^{-C(t-s)}F_{t}(s,x)\,\mathrm{d}s.

Integration by parts in time therefore yields

(6.39)

H(t,x)\geq e^{-Ct}\rho_{0}(x)-F(t,x)+e^{-Ct}F(0,x)+C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-C(t-s)}F(s,x)\,\mathrm{d}s.

Since

C\int_{0}^{t}e^{-C(t-s)}\,\mathrm{d}s=1-e^{-Ct}\leq 1,\qquad|e^{-Ct}F(0,x)|\leq\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|F(t,x)|,

we obtain from (
6.39
) that

H(t,x)\geq e^{-CT}\rho_{0}(x)-3\sup_{0\leq t\leq T}|F(t,x)|.

Combining this inequality with (
6.38
) and
q=1+\frac{1+\alpha}{\alpha}
, we obtain from
\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\sim d(x)
that

H(t,x)\geq\rho_{0}(x)\big(e^{-CT}-3C(T)d(x)^{\frac{\alpha}{1+2\alpha}}\big)\qquad\text{for }(t,x)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}.

Now, set

b_{T}=\min\Big\{\frac{1}{6},\Big(\frac{e^{-CT}}{6C(T)}\Big)^{\frac{1+2\alpha}{\alpha}}\Big\}.

Then
b_{T}>0
depends only on
(\alpha,\varepsilon_{0},|I|,\rho_{0},u_{0},T)
and, for
t\in[0,T]
and
0\leq d(x)\leq b_{T}
(that is,
x\in[0,b_{T}]\cup[1-b_{T},1]
), we have

H(t,x)\geq\frac{1}{2}e^{-CT}\rho_{0}(x).

Clearly, using equation
H=\frac{\rho_{0}}{\eta_{x}}
, we conclude that

(6.40)

\eta_{x}(t,x)\leq 2e^{CT}=:C(T)\qquad\text{for }t\in[0,T],\quad x\in[0,b_{T}]\cup[1-b_{T},1].

Finally, taking
b=b_{T}
in (
6.37
), combined with (
6.40
), yields the desired estimate.
∎

7.
Global-in-time weighted energy estimates on the velocity

In this section, we aim to establish the global-in-time weighted estimates of
U
. The tangential estimates are derived in §
7.1
, and the elliptic estimates are derived in §
7.2
-§
7.3
.

7.1.
Tangential estimates on the velocity

We first give a lemma to build a bridge between the spatial derivatives and the temporal ones.

Lemma 7.1
.

For any
T>0
,
0<\alpha\leq 1
and
0\leq\iota<3\alpha+1
, it holds that

\displaystyle\left|U_{x}(t,x)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\rho_{0}(x)+C(T)\rho_{0}(x)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2};

\displaystyle\left|U_{x}(t,x)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\rho_{0}(x)+C(\iota,T)\rho_{0}(x)^{\frac{3\alpha-1-\iota}{2}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}-\alpha}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2};

\displaystyle\left|U_{tx}(t,x)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C\rho_{0}^{2}(x)+C(\iota,T)\big(\rho_{0}(x)^{3\alpha-1-\iota}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}-\alpha}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\rho_{0}(x)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(t)\big|_{2}\big),

for all
(t,x)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}
.

Proof.

Integrating
\eqref{secondreformulation}_{1}
over
[0,x]
for
0\leq x\leq\frac{1}{2}
yields

(7.1)

U_{x}(t,x)=\rho_{0}(x)+\eta_{x}^{2}\rho_{0}^{-1}\int_{0}^{x}\rho_{0}(z)U_{t}(t,z)\,\mathrm{d}z.

Then it follows from
\rho_{0}\sim d(x)^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}
, Lemma
6.11
and Hölder’s inequality that

(7.2)

\displaystyle\left|U_{x}(t,x)\right|

\displaystyle\leq Cx^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}+C(T)x^{-\frac{1}{\alpha}}\left(\int_{0}^{x}z^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}\,\mathrm{d}z\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}\left(\int\rho_{0}U_{t}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}z\right)^{\frac{1}{2}}

\displaystyle\leq Cd(x)^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}+C(T)d(x)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}.

One can perform the same calculation for
\frac{1}{2}<x\leq 1
by integrating
\eqref{secondreformulation}_{1}
over
[x,1]
to get (
7.2
).

Similarly, it also holds that

(7.3)

\left|U_{x}(t,x)\right|\leq Cd(x)^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}+C(\iota,T)d(x)^{\frac{3\alpha-1-\iota}{2\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}-\alpha}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2},

for all
(t,x)\in[0,T]\times\bar{I}
, where
0\leq\iota<3\alpha+1
.

Finally, differentiating (
7.1
) with respect to
t
gives

U_{tx}(t,x)=2\eta_{x}U_{x}\rho_{0}^{-1}\int_{0}^{x}\rho_{0}(z)U_{t}(t,z)\,\mathrm{d}z+\eta_{x}^{2}\rho_{0}^{-1}\int_{0}^{x}\rho_{0}(z)U_{tt}(t,z)\,\mathrm{d}z,

which, together with an analogous computation, (
7.3
) and Young’s inequality, leads to

\displaystyle\left|U_{tx}(t,x)\right|

\displaystyle\leq C(\iota,T)\left|U_{x}(t,x)\right|d(x)^{\frac{3\alpha-1-\iota}{2\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}-\alpha}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}+C(T)d(x)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(t)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq Cd(x)^{\frac{2}{\alpha}}+C(\iota,T)d(x)^{\frac{3\alpha-1-\iota}{\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}-\alpha}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+C(T)d(x)^{\frac{\alpha-1}{2\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(t)\big|_{2}.

The proof of Lemma
7.1
is completed.
∎

Now, we are going to derive the tangential estimates in the following three lemmas.

Lemma 7.2
.

For any
T>0
,
0<\alpha\leq 1
and
2\leq p<\infty
, it holds that

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta}{p}}U(t)\big|_{p}^{p}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\left|U\right|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\beta,p,T),

for all
0\leq t\leq T
, where
\beta>\alpha
if
0<\alpha<1
and
\beta=1
if
\alpha=1
.

Proof.

One notes that, due to Lemmas
6.3
and
6.11
, it remains only to show the case for
\beta>\alpha
,
0<\alpha<1
. To this end, using (
6.1
), one can rewrite
\eqref{lagrange}_{2}
as follows:

(7.4)

\rho_{0}U_{t}-\left(\frac{\rho_{0}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\right)_{x}+2\frac{\rho_{0}^{2}}{\eta_{x}}(V-U)=0.

Multiply (
7.4
) by
\rho_{0}^{\beta-1}\left|U\right|^{p-2}U
and integrate the resulting equality over
I
to get

(7.5)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{p}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int\rho_{0}^{\beta}\left|U\right|^{p}\,\mathrm{d}x+(p-1)\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{\beta}\left|U\right|^{p-2}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\frac{1-\beta}{\alpha}\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{\beta-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\left|U\right|^{p-2}UU_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x-2\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{\beta+1}}{\eta_{x}}(V-U)\left|U\right|^{p-2}U\,\mathrm{d}x:=\sum_{i=1}^{2}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

Then it follows from
\beta>\alpha
, Lemmas
6.4
and
6.6
-
6.7
, Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{1}

\displaystyle=\frac{1-\beta}{\alpha}\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{\beta-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\left|U\right|^{p-2}UU_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C(\beta,T)\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta-2\alpha}{p}}\eta_{x}^{-\frac{2}{p}}U\big|_{p}^{\frac{p}{2}}\Big|\frac{\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\left|U\right|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}

(7.6)

\displaystyle\leq C(\beta,p,T)+\frac{p-1}{8}\Big|\frac{\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta}{2}}\left|U\right|^{\frac{p-2}{2}}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{2}

\displaystyle=-2\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{\beta+1}}{\eta_{x}}(V-U)\left|U\right|^{p-2}U\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta+1-\alpha}{p-1}}U\big|_{p-1}^{p-1}+2\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\beta+1}{p}}U\big|_{p}^{p}\big)\leq C(\beta,p,T),

which, along with (
7.5
), Lemma
6.11
and Grönwall’s inequality, yields the conclusion.

The proof of Lemma
7.2
is completed.
∎

Lemma 7.3
.

For any
T>0
and
0<\alpha\leq 1
, it holds that

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{x}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T.

Proof.

Step 1.

First, multiplying (
7.4
) by
\eta_{x}\rho_{0}^{\frac{\varepsilon-1}{2}}(\varepsilon>0)
, along with (
6.1
), one gets that

(7.7)

\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}=\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}\eta_{x}U_{t}+2\rho_{0}^{\frac{3+\varepsilon}{2}}(V-U)-\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}(V-U)U_{x},

which, along with Lemmas
6.5
,
6.7
,
6.11
and
7.1
, leads to

(7.8)

\displaystyle\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{\varepsilon}{2\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}+C|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}|_{\infty}^{\frac{3-\alpha}{2\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}V\big|_{2}+C\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3+\varepsilon}{2}}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+C\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}V\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U\big|_{2}\big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big).

Step 2.
Multiplying (
7.4
) by
U_{t}
and integrating the resulting equality over
I
yield

(7.9)

\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int\frac{\rho_{0}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x+\int\rho_{0}U_{t}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x=-\int\frac{\rho_{0}U_{x}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}x-2\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{2}(V-U)U_{t}}{\eta_{x}}\,\mathrm{d}x:=\sum_{i=3}^{4}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{3}
, if
0<\alpha<1
, it follows from integration by parts, (
6.1
), (
7.8
), Lemmas
6.4
,
6.6
and
A.5
, Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality that for any
\varepsilon>0
,

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{3}

\displaystyle=-\int\frac{\rho_{0}U_{x}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}x=\left.-\frac{\rho_{0}UU_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\right|_{x=0}^{x=1}+\int H_{x}\frac{UU_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x+2\int\frac{\rho_{0}UU_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=\int\rho_{0}(V-U)\frac{UU_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x+2\int\frac{\rho_{0}UU_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x

(7.10)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}-\alpha}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\varepsilon}{2}}UU_{x}\big|_{2}+C(T)\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{\varepsilon}{\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\varepsilon}{2}}UU_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\varepsilon}{2}}UU_{x}\big|_{2}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\Big(1+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\varepsilon}{2}}|U|^{j}U_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}\Big)+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2};

while, if
\alpha=1
, it follows from Lemmas
6.4
and
7.1
, and Young’s inequality that

(7.11)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{3}

\displaystyle=-\int\frac{\rho_{0}U_{x}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}x\leq C(T)\left|U_{x}\right|_{\infty}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\big)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}\big)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{4}
, it follows from Lemmas
6.4
and
6.6
-
6.7
, Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality that

(7.12)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{4}

\displaystyle=-2\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{2}(V-U)U_{t}}{\eta_{x}}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{3}{2\alpha}-1}\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}U\big|_{2}\big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}.

Substituting (
7.10
)-(
7.12
) into (
7.9
) and choosing
0<\varepsilon<1-\alpha
in (
7.10
) when
0<\alpha<1
, one can get from the Grönwall inequality, Lemmas
6.11
and
7.2
that

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{x}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T.

The proof of Lemma
7.3
is completed.
∎

Lemma 7.4
.

For any
T>0
and
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, it holds that for all
0\leq t\leq T
,

\sum_{j=1}^{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{j}U(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

Proof.

Step 1: Estimate of
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}
.

First, Lemma
7.1
implies that

(7.13)

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{4}}U_{x}(t)\big|_{4}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{5}{4\alpha}}+C(T)\left(\int\rho_{0}^{2\alpha-1}\,\mathrm{d}x\right)^{\frac{1}{4}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}\big),

where one has used the facts that
\alpha>\frac{1}{3}
and
\rho_{0}\sim d(x)^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}
.

Next, applying
\partial_{t}
to both sides of
\eqref{secondreformulation}_{1}
shows that

(7.14)

\rho_{0}U_{tt}-\left(\frac{\rho_{0}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\right)_{x}=\left(\frac{2\rho_{0}^{2}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}-\frac{2\rho_{0}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\right)_{x}.

Multiplying (
7.14
) by
U_{t}
and integrating the resulting equality over
I
lead to

(7.15)

\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int\rho_{0}U_{t}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x+\int\frac{\rho_{0}U_{tx}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x=\int\frac{2\rho_{0}U_{x}^{2}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}x-\int\frac{2\rho_{0}^{2}U_{x}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}x:=\sum_{i=5}^{6}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{5}
-
\mathcal{G}_{6}
, since
\alpha>\frac{1}{3}
, it follows from Lemmas
6.4
and
7.3
, (
7.13
), Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{5}

\displaystyle=\int\frac{2\rho_{0}U_{x}^{2}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}x\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{4}}U_{x}\big|_{4}^{2}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}\big)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}

(7.16)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{4}\big)+\frac{1}{8}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{6}

\displaystyle=-\int\frac{2\rho_{0}^{2}U_{x}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{2}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{8}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}^{2}.

Thus, it follows from (
7.15
)-(
7.16
), Grönwall’s inequality, and Lemmas
6.4
and
7.3
that

(7.17)

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T,

which, along with (
7.8
), implies that for all
\varepsilon>0
,

(7.18)

\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon}{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

Step 2: Estimate of
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}
.
First, since
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, it follows from (
7.17
), Lemmas
7.1
and
A.5
, and
\rho_{0}\sim d(x)^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}
that

(7.19)

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\iota}{2}}U_{x}(t)\big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{3-3\alpha+\iota}{2\alpha}}+C(\iota,T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\iota}{2}-\alpha}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\iota)+C(\iota,T)\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{\iota}{2\alpha}}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{j}U_{t}(t)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\iota,T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}\big),

where
\iota
satisfies
0\leq\iota<3\alpha+1
if
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<1
and
0<\iota<4
if
\alpha=1
. However, since
\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\in L^{\infty}
, (
7.19
) actually holds for all
\iota\geq 0
when
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<1
and for all
\iota>0
when
\alpha=1
.

Next, multiplying (
7.14
) by
U_{tt}
and then integrating the resulting equality over
I
yield

(7.20)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int\rho_{0}\frac{U_{tx}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x+\int\rho_{0}U_{tt}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int\rho_{0}\frac{U_{x}U_{tx}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}x-\int\left(\frac{2\rho_{0}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\right)_{x}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x+\int\left(\frac{2\rho_{0}^{2}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\right)_{x}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x:=\sum_{i=7}^{9}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{7}
-
\mathcal{G}_{9}
, since
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, choosing
\iota=0
if
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<1
and
\iota=1
if
\alpha=1
, one gets from (
6.1
), Lemmas
6.4
,
6.5
-
6.7
,
7.3
, and
A.5
, (
7.17
)-(
7.19
), Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{7}

\displaystyle=-\int\rho_{0}\frac{U_{x}U_{tx}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\,\mathrm{d}x\leq C(T)\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{3\alpha-1-\iota}{2\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\iota}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}^{2}\big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{8}

\displaystyle=-\int\left(\frac{2\rho_{0}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\right)_{x}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=-2\int H_{x}\frac{U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x-4\int H\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=-2\int\rho_{0}(V-U)\frac{U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x-4\int\frac{\rho_{0}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x

(7.21)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{3\alpha-\frac{1}{2}-\iota}V\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{3\alpha-\frac{1}{2}-\iota}U\big|_{2}\big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\iota}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}^{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\iota}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha-\iota}{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\iota}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}^{2}\big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\iota}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}^{2}+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}^{4}\big)+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{9}

\displaystyle=\int\left(\frac{2\rho_{0}^{2}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\right)_{x}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x=4\int HH_{x}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x+2\int H^{2}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=4\int\rho_{0}^{2}(V-U)\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x+2\int\frac{\rho_{0}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}U_{tt}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\left(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U\right|_{\infty}\right)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}+C(T)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}+\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}\Big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}.

Then it follows from (
7.20
)-(
7.21
) and Lemma
6.11
that

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(T)+C(T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}^{2}\big)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\frac{U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}^{2},

which, along with the Grönwall inequality, Lemma
6.11
and (
7.17
), yields that

(7.22)

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T.

Step 3: Estimate of
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}
.

First, it follows from (
7.19
) and (
7.22
) that

(7.23)

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\iota}{2}}U_{x}(t)\big|_{\infty}\leq C(\iota,T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}(t)\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(\iota,T),

for all
0\leq t\leq T
, where
\iota\geq 0
if
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<1
and
\iota>0
if
\alpha=1
.

Next, one can formally apply
\partial_{t}
to both sides of (
7.14
) to obtain that

(7.24)

\rho_{0}\partial_{t}^{3}U-\left(\frac{\rho_{0}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\right)_{x}=\left(\frac{2\rho_{0}^{2}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}-\frac{6\rho_{0}^{2}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{4}}-\frac{6\rho_{0}U_{x}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}+\frac{6\rho_{0}U_{x}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{4}}\right)_{x}.

Multiplying (
7.24
) by
U_{tt}
and integrating the resulting equality over
I
imply that

(7.25)

\displaystyle\frac{1}{2}\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\int\rho_{0}U_{tt}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}x+\int\rho_{0}\frac{(\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x})^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle-\int\frac{2\rho_{0}^{2}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x+\int\frac{6\rho_{0}^{2}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{4}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x+\int\frac{6\rho_{0}U_{x}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle-\int\frac{6\rho_{0}U_{x}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{4}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x:=\sum_{i=10}^{13}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

It should be noted that the above energy equality can be verified by the standard functional method. Indeed, using the notations given at the beginning of §
3
, for a.e.
t\in(0,T)
and all test functions
\varphi\in C^{\infty}(\bar{I})
, one can get from (
7.14
) that

\langle\rho_{0}U_{tt},\varphi\rangle=\left<-\frac{\rho_{0}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}-\frac{2\rho_{0}^{2}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}+\frac{2\rho_{0}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{3}},\varphi_{x}\right>,

which, together with the facts that
U
satisfies (
1.25
) and
\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\in L^{2}([0,T];L^{2}_{\rho_{0}})
, leads to

\displaystyle\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\langle\rho_{0}U_{tt},\varphi\rangle

\displaystyle=\left<-\frac{\rho_{0}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}-\frac{2\rho_{0}^{2}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}+\frac{6\rho_{0}^{2}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{4}}+\frac{6\rho_{0}U_{x}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}-\frac{6\rho_{0}U_{x}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{4}},\varphi_{x}\right>

\displaystyle\leq\big(C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}(t)\big|_{2}+A_{1}(t)\big)\left\|\varphi\right\|_{1,\rho_{0}}\leq A_{2}(t)\left\|\varphi\right\|_{1,\rho_{0}},

for some positive functions
A_{1}(t),A_{2}(t)\in L^{2}(0,T)
. Thus, it follows from Lemma
A.1
and the Lemma 1.1 on page 250 of
[
55
]
that
(\rho_{0}U_{tt})_{t}=\rho_{0}\partial_{t}^{3}U\in L^{2}([0,T];H^{-1}_{\rho_{0}})
and

\frac{\mathrm{d}}{\mathrm{d}t}\langle\rho_{0}U_{tt},U_{tt}\rangle=2\langle\rho_{0}\partial_{t}^{3}U,U_{tt}\rangle_{H^{-1}_{\rho_{0}}\times H^{1}_{\rho_{0}}}.

Consequently, one can deduce (
7.25
) from the above formula.

For
\mathcal{G}_{10}
-
\mathcal{G}_{13}
, due to
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, setting
\iota=0
if
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<1
and
\iota=1
if
\alpha=1
, then one gets from Lemmas
6.4
and
7.3
, (
7.22
)-(
7.23
), Hölder’s inequality and Young’s inequality that

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{10}

\displaystyle=-\int\frac{2\rho_{0}^{2}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{x}^{-1}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{11}

\displaystyle=\int\frac{6\rho_{0}^{2}U_{x}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{4}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\iota}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha+2-\iota}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{x}^{-1}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\big|_{2}^{2},

(7.26)

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{12}

\displaystyle=\int\frac{6\rho_{0}U_{x}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\iota}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha-\iota}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{x}^{-1}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\big|_{2}^{2},

\displaystyle\mathcal{G}_{13}

\displaystyle=-\int\frac{6\rho_{0}U_{x}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{4}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\,\mathrm{d}x

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\iota}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}^{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{3\alpha-\iota-\frac{1}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\big|_{2}\leq C(T)+\frac{1}{8}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{x}^{-1}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}.

Thus, according to (
7.25
)-(
7.26
) and Grönwall’s inequality, one can get

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}(t)\big|_{2}^{2}+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}^{2}U_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T.

The proof of Lemma
7.4
is completed.
∎

7.2.
The second and third order elliptic estimates on the velocity

We first prove the following estimates.

Lemma 7.5
.

For any
T>0
,
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
and
\varepsilon>0
, it holds that for all
0\leq t\leq T
,

\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}(t)\Big|_{2}+\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{xx}(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

Proof.

Step 1: Estimate of
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\left(U_{x}\eta_{x}^{-1}\right)_{x}
.

Multiplying (
7.7
) by
\rho_{0}^{\frac{\varepsilon}{2}-\alpha}
(
\varepsilon>0
) gives

(7.27)

\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}=\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\eta_{x}U_{t}+2\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}(V-U)-\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}(V-U)U_{x},

which, along with (
7.23
), Lemmas
6.5
,
6.7
,
6.11
,
7.4
and
A.5
, yields that for all
0\leq t\leq T
,

(7.28)

\displaystyle\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}U_{t}\big|_{2}+2\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}V\big|_{2}+2\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle+\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}V\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U\big|_{2}\big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}U_{t}\big|_{2}+C(\varepsilon,T)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}U_{t}\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}U_{tx}\big|_{2}\big)+C(\varepsilon,T)\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

Step 2: Estimate of
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\left(U_{x}\eta_{x}^{-1}\right)_{xx}
.

Multiplying
\eqref{lagrange}_{2}
by
\frac{1}{H}
and then applying
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\partial_{x}
(
\varepsilon>0
) to the resulting equality, one gets

(7.29)

\displaystyle\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{xx}

\displaystyle=\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}(\eta_{x}U_{t})_{x}+2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}H_{xx}

\displaystyle-\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\frac{H_{x}}{H}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}-\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\left(\frac{H_{x}}{H}\right)_{x}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}:=\sum_{i=14}^{17}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{14}
, since
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, according to (
6.1
), Lemmas
6.6
,
6.11
,
7.4
and
A.5
, one has

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{14}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}(\eta_{x}U_{t})_{x}\big|_{2}=\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\eta_{xx}U_{t}+\eta_{x}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}U_{tx}\Big|_{2}

(7.30)

\displaystyle=\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\eta_{x}^{2}(U-V)U_{t}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\eta_{x}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{t}+\eta_{x}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}U_{tx}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\left(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U\right|_{\infty}+\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\right)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}U_{t}\big|_{2}+C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}U_{tx}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}U\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{2}+1\big)\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{t}\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

For
\mathcal{G}_{15}
, it follows from (
6.1
), Lemmas
6.5
and
6.7
-
6.8
and
7.3
that

(7.31)

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{15}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\big|2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}H_{xx}\big|_{2}=\Big|\frac{2}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}(V-U)+2\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}+\varepsilon}(V_{x}-U_{x})\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}V\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}U\big|_{2}\big)+2\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}+\varepsilon}V_{x}\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}+\varepsilon}U_{x}\big|_{2}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

For
\mathcal{G}_{16}
, it follows from
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, (
6.1
), (
7.28
), Lemmas
6.6
-
6.7
,
6.11
,
7.3
and
A.5
that

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{16}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\frac{H_{x}}{H}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}=\Big|\eta_{x}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}(V-U)\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}

(7.32)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\left(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U\right|_{\infty}\right)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\left(1+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U\right|_{\infty}\right)

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}U\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

At last, for
\mathcal{G}_{17}
, it follows from
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, (
6.1
), Lemmas
6.5
-
6.8
,
6.11
,
7.3
,
A.5
and (
7.23
) that

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{17}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\left(\frac{H_{x}}{H}\right)_{x}\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}(V_{x}-U_{x})U_{x}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}(V-U)U_{x}-\eta_{x}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}(V-U)^{2}U_{x}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}V_{x}\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{2}\big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}

(7.33)

\displaystyle\quad+C\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}V\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U\big|_{2}\big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\left(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U\right|_{\infty}\right)\big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}V\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U\big|_{2}\big)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)(1+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U\right|_{\infty})

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}U\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

Therefore, collecting (
7.29
)-(
7.33
) leads to

(7.34)

\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{xx}(t)\Big|_{2}\leq C(\varepsilon,T),

for all
0\leq t\leq T
,
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
and
\varepsilon>0
. The proof of Lemma
7.5
is completed.
∎

Now one can improve the regularities of
\eta_{x}
with the help of Lemma
7.5
.

Corollary 7.1
.

For any
T>0
,
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
and
\varepsilon>0
, it holds that for all
0\leq t\leq T
,

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\eta_{xx}(t)\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}+\varepsilon}\eta_{xx}(t)\big|_{\infty}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

Proof.

Note that

(7.35)

\begin{gathered}\eta_{xx}=\eta_{x}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\,\mathrm{d}s,\quad\partial_{x}^{3}\eta=\frac{\eta_{xx}^{2}}{\eta_{x}}+\eta_{x}\int_{0}^{t}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{xx}\,\mathrm{d}s.\end{gathered}

Multiplying the first identity in (
7.35
) by
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}
and
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}+\varepsilon}
, respectively, then according to Lemmas
6.11
,
7.5
and
A.5
, one can get

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\eta_{xx}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\varepsilon,T),

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}+\varepsilon}\eta_{xx}\big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}+\varepsilon}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{\infty}\,\mathrm{d}s

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\sum_{j=1}^{2}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{j}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)\Big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

Consequently, multiplying the second identity in (
7.35
) by
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}
, one gets from the above estimates, Lemmas
6.4
,
6.11
and
7.5
that

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1+\varepsilon-\alpha}{2}}\eta_{xx}\big|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha}{2}}\eta_{xx}\big|_{2}+C(T)\int_{0}^{t}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{xx}\Big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

Therefore, the proof of Corollary
7.1
is completed.
∎

Using Lemma
7.5
and Corollary
7.1
, one can deduce the following elliptic estimates.

Lemma 7.6
.

For any
T>0
,
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
and
\varepsilon>0
, it holds that

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}U_{xx}(t)\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{3}U(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T.

Proof.

Step 1: Estimate of
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}U_{xx}
.

Note that

U_{xx}=\eta_{x}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}+\frac{U_{x}\eta_{xx}}{\eta_{x}}.

Then, for all
\varepsilon>0
,
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, it follows from (
7.23
), Lemmas
6.4
,
6.11
and
7.5
, and Corollary
7.1
that

(7.36)

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}U_{xx}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\eta_{x}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}+\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\frac{U_{x}\eta_{xx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}+C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}\eta_{xx}\big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}\big)\leq C(\varepsilon,T),

where one has used the fact that
\frac{\alpha}{2}>\frac{1}{2}-\alpha
if
\alpha>\frac{1}{3}
.

Step 2: Estimate of
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{3}U
.

Note that

\partial_{x}^{3}U=\eta_{x}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{xx}+2\eta_{xx}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}+\frac{U_{x}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta}{\eta_{x}}.

Then for all
\varepsilon>0
,
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, it follows from (
7.23
), Lemmas
6.4
,
6.11
,
7.5
and
A.5
, and Corollary
7.1
that

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{3}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\eta_{x}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{xx}+2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\eta_{xx}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}+\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\frac{U_{x}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{xx}\Big|_{2}+C\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}\eta_{xx}\big|_{\infty}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}

(7.37)

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta\big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T)\Big(1+\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}+\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha+\varepsilon}{2}}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{xx}\Big|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(\varepsilon,T).

The proof of Lemma
7.6
is completed.
∎

Lemma 7.7
.

For any
T>0
and
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, it holds that

\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{xx}(t)\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}U(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T,

where
\varepsilon_{0}
is defined as in (
1.18
).

Proof.

Step 1: Estimate of
\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{xx}
.

This is a direct consequence of Lemma
7.6
. Indeed, define
\varepsilon_{0}
as in (
1.18
) and set
\varepsilon>0
as

\varepsilon=\Big(\frac{5}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\Big)\alpha-\frac{1}{2}>0.

Then one has
\frac{1}{2}+\varepsilon-\alpha=\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha
, and hence according to Lemma
7.6
, it holds that

(7.38)

\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{xx}(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T.

Step 2: Estimate of
\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}U
.

If
0<\varepsilon_{0}<\frac{3\alpha-1}{2\alpha}
,
0<\alpha\leq 1
, one can set
\varepsilon>0
in Lemma
7.6
as

\varepsilon:=\Big(\frac{3\alpha-1}{2\alpha}-\varepsilon_{0}\Big)\alpha>0.

Then the conclusion holds automatically. Thus, it suffices to show the case when
\varepsilon_{0}=\frac{3\alpha-1}{2\alpha}
and establish the following estimate:

(7.39)

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}U(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T.

Actually, for the parameter
\varepsilon_{0}
defined in (
1.18
), if
\varepsilon_{0}=\frac{3\alpha-1}{2\alpha}
, then it holds that
\frac{3\alpha-1}{2\alpha}<\frac{1}{\alpha}-1
, i.e.,
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<\frac{3}{5}
; otherwise, one has
\varepsilon_{0}<\frac{1}{\alpha}-1\leq\frac{3\alpha-1}{2\alpha}
, and then the desired estimate holds automatically as discussed above. Thus, it suffices to show (
7.39
) under
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<\frac{3}{5}
.

To get (
7.39
) under
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<\frac{3}{5}
, one may follow the proofs in (
3.87
). Multiplying
\eqref{secondreformulation}_{1}
by
\eta_{x}^{2}\rho_{0}^{\alpha-1}
and applying
\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}\partial_{x}
to the resulting equality, one gets

\displaystyle\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}U+\Big(\frac{1}{\alpha}+1\Big)\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{xx}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underline{-\frac{1}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}U_{x}+\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{x}^{2}U_{tx}+2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{x}\eta_{xx}U_{t}}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{18}}

(7.40)

\displaystyle\underline{+\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{x}^{2}U_{t}+\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{xx}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}+\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{xx}U_{xx}}{\eta_{x}}}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{19}}

\displaystyle\underline{+\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}-\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{xx}^{2}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}-\frac{2+2\alpha}{\alpha}\frac{\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{xx}}{\eta_{x}}}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{20}}

\displaystyle\underline{-\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta}{\eta_{x}}+\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}\eta_{xx}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}+\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}-2\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}^{2}+\frac{2}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{21}}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{18}
-
\mathcal{G}_{19}
, it follows from (
7.23
), Lemmas
6.4
,
6.11
,
7.3
-
7.4
,
7.6
and
A.5
, and Corollary
7.1
that

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{18}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|-\frac{1}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}U_{x}+\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{x}^{2}U_{tx}+2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{x}\eta_{xx}U_{t}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}U_{x}\big|_{2}+C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}+C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}+\varepsilon}\eta_{xx}\big|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha}{2}-\varepsilon}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\sum_{j=1}^{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{j}U\big|_{2}+C(T)\bigg(1+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}-\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{j}U_{t}\big|_{2}\bigg)\leq C(T),

(7.41)

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{19}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{x}^{2}U_{t}+\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{xx}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}+\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{xx}U_{xx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}U_{t}\big|_{2}+C(T)\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{j}(\eta_{xx}U_{x})\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}+\varepsilon}\eta_{xx}\big|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{\alpha}{2}-\varepsilon}U_{xx}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\bigg(\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{j}U_{t}\big|_{2}+\sum_{j=1}^{3}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}-\varepsilon}\partial_{x}^{j}U\big|_{2}+1\bigg)\leq C(T),

where
\varepsilon
shall be chosen such that
0<\varepsilon<\frac{3\alpha-1}{2}
.

For
\mathcal{G}_{20}
-
\mathcal{G}_{21}
, setting
\varepsilon
as above, one gets from (
7.23
), Lemma
6.4
, Corollary
7.1
and
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<\frac{3}{5}
that

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{20}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}-\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{xx}^{2}U_{x}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}-\frac{2+2\alpha}{\alpha}\frac{\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{xx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\left|U_{x}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}+\varepsilon}\eta_{xx}\big|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha+\varepsilon}\eta_{xx}\big|_{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha-1}{2}-2\varepsilon}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}\eta_{xx}\big|_{2}

(7.42)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big),

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{21}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle=\Big|-\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta}{\eta_{x}}+\frac{2\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}}\eta_{xx}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}+\frac{2}{\alpha^{2}}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}-2\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}^{2}+\frac{2}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\frac{3}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\Big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta\big|_{2}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{2\alpha}}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\eta_{xx}\big|_{\infty}^{2}\big)

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\big(\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}^{2}\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{3-4\alpha}{2\alpha}}+\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{3-2\alpha}{2\alpha}}\big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big).

Collecting (
7.40
)-(
7.42
) shows that for any
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<\frac{3}{5}
,

\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}U+\Big(\frac{1}{\alpha}+1\Big)\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{xx}\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big),

for all
0\leq t\leq T
, which, along with Proposition
C.1
and (
7.38
), yields that

(7.43)

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}U(t)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big(\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}U+\Big(\frac{1}{\alpha}+1\Big)\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{xx}\Big|_{2}+\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{xx}\big|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big).

Finally, it follows from (
7.43
) and Grönwall’s inequality that (
7.39
) holds.

The proof of Lemma
7.7
is completed.
∎

It follows from Lemmas
7.7
and
A.5
that the following corollary holds.

Corollary 7.2
.

For any
T>0
and
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, it holds that for all
0\leq t\leq T
,

\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\eta_{xx}(t)\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(1-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\eta_{xx}(t)\big|_{\infty}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(T),

where
\varepsilon_{0}
is defined as in (
1.18
).

Proof.

Note that Lemma
7.7
yields

(7.44)

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\eta_{xx}(t)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{xx}\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T),

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta(t)\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(T).

In addition, (
7.44
) and Lemma
A.5
imply that

\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\eta_{xx}\big|_{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(1-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\eta_{xx}\big|_{\infty}\leq C\sum_{j=2}^{3}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{j}\eta\big|_{2}\leq C(T).

The proof of Corollary
7.2
is completed.
∎

7.3.
The fourth order elliptic estimates on the velocity

Lemma 7.8
.

For any
T>0
and
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, it holds that

\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{t}U_{xx}(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T,

where
\varepsilon_{0}
is defined as in (
1.18
).

Proof.

Applying
\eta_{x}^{2}\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha-1}\partial_{t}
to both sides of
\eqref{secondreformulation}_{1}
, along with (
6.1
), yields

(7.45)

\displaystyle\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{t}U_{xx}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{tx}

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\eta_{x}^{2}\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{tt}-4\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha+1}(V-U)U_{x}-2\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha+1}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}

\displaystyle+2\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}(V-U)U_{x}^{2}+4\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{x}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}+2\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\frac{\eta_{xx}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}}:=\sum_{i=22}^{27}\mathcal{G}_{i}.

First, for
\mathcal{G}_{22}
-
\mathcal{G}_{26}
, it follows from (
7.23
), Lemmas
6.5
-
6.7
,
6.11
,
7.1
-
7.5
and
A.5
, and Corollary
7.2
that

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{22}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\leq C(T),

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{23}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U\right|_{\infty}\right)\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha+1}U_{x}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\partial_{x}^{j}U\big|_{2}\bigg)\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha+1}U_{x}\big|_{2}\leq C(T),

(7.46)

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{24}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right|_{\infty}^{\frac{1}{\alpha}}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}\leq C(T),

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{25}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U\right|_{\infty}\right)\left|U_{x}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{x}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\bigg(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}V\right|_{\infty}+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\partial_{x}^{j}U\big|_{2}\bigg)\left|U_{x}\right|_{\infty}\sum_{j=1}^{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{j}U\big|_{2}\leq C(T),

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{26}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left|U_{x}\right|_{\infty}\Big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\left(\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}\right)_{x}\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).

Next, for
\mathcal{G}_{27}
, since
\alpha>\frac{1}{3}
, according to Lemmas
7.1
,
7.4
and
A.5
, choosing
0<\iota\leq\frac{5\alpha-1}{2}
in Lemma
7.1
, one gets

(7.47)

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1-\alpha}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{\infty}\leq C(T)\bigg(1+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{j}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\bigg)\leq C(T).

Thus, it follows from Lemma
6.4
, Corollary
7.2
, (
7.47
) and
\alpha>\frac{1-\alpha}{2}
that

(7.48)

\left|\mathcal{G}_{27}\right|_{2}=\Big|2\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\frac{\eta_{xx}U_{tx}}{\eta_{x}}\Big|_{2}\leq C\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\eta_{xx}\big|_{2}\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U_{tx}\right|_{\infty}\leq C(T).

Then, according to (
7.45
)-(
7.46
) and (
7.48
), one has

(7.49)

\Big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{t}U_{xx}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{tx}\Big|_{2}\leq C(T).

Moreover, if
0<\iota<3\alpha+1
and
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, it follows from Lemmas
7.1
,
7.4
and
A.5
that

(7.50)

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha+\iota}U_{tx}\big|_{2}\leq C(\iota,T)\Big(1+\sum_{j=0}^{1}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{x}^{j}U_{t}\big|_{2}^{2}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tt}\big|_{2}\Big)\leq C(\iota,T).

Since
\rho_{0}\in L^{\infty}
, (
7.50
) actually holds for all
\iota>0
and
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
. Hence, if
0<\varepsilon_{0}<\frac{3\alpha-1}{2\alpha}
, one can set
\iota=\frac{3\alpha-1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\alpha
in (
7.50
), and get from (
7.49
) that

(7.51)

\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{t}U_{xx}\big|_{2}\leq C(T);

while, if
\varepsilon_{0}=\frac{3\alpha-1}{2\alpha}
, (
7.49
) can be reduced to

(7.52)

\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}U_{xx}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{tx}\Big|_{2}\leq C(T),

which, along with Proposition
C.1
and Lemma
7.4
, implies that

(7.53)

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}U_{xx}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}\partial_{t}U_{xx}+\frac{1}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}-\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}U_{tx}\Big|_{2}+C\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}U_{tx}\big|_{2}\leq C(T).

The proof of Lemma
7.8
is completed.
∎

Lemma 7.9
.

For any
T>0
and
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha\leq 1
, it holds that

\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T,

where
\varepsilon_{0}
is defined as in (
1.18
).

Proof.

Similar to the derivation of (
3.92
), one can replace
\bar{\eta}
in (
3.92
) with
\eta
to obtain that

\displaystyle\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U+\Big(\frac{1}{\alpha}+2\Big)\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\partial_{x}^{3}U

\displaystyle=

\displaystyle\underline{\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\eta_{x}^{2}\partial_{t}U_{xx}+2\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\left((\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{x}+2\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\eta_{xx}\right)\eta_{x}U_{tx}+2\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\left(\eta_{xx}^{2}+\eta_{x}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta\right)U_{t}}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{28}}

\displaystyle\underline{+\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\left((\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\eta_{x}+4(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{xx}\right)\eta_{x}U_{t}-\Big(\frac{2}{\alpha}+1\Big)\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}U_{xx}}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{29}}

\displaystyle\underline{-\frac{1}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U_{x}+2\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\Big((\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\eta_{xx}+2(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta-\frac{2(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{xx}^{2}}{\eta_{x}}\Big)\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{30}}

(7.54)

\displaystyle\underline{+2\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\Big(\partial_{x}^{4}\eta-\frac{3\eta_{xx}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta}{\eta_{x}}+\frac{2\eta_{xx}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\Big)\frac{U_{x}}{\eta_{x}}}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{31}}

\displaystyle\underline{+4\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\Big(\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta+(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{xx}-\frac{\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\eta_{xx}^{2}}{\eta_{x}}\Big)\frac{U_{xx}}{\eta_{x}}+2\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\frac{\eta_{xx}\partial_{x}^{3}U}{\eta_{x}}}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{32}}

\displaystyle\underline{-\frac{2+2\alpha}{\alpha^{2}}\frac{\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}^{2}\eta_{xx}}{\eta_{x}}-2\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha+1}\Big(\frac{\partial_{x}^{4}\eta}{\eta_{x}}-\frac{3\eta_{xx}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta}{\eta_{x}^{2}}+\frac{2\eta_{xx}^{3}}{\eta_{x}^{3}}\Big)}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{33}}

\displaystyle\underline{-\frac{2+2\alpha}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha+1}\Big(\frac{(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\eta_{xx}}{\eta_{x}}+\frac{2(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta}{\eta_{x}}-\frac{2(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\eta_{xx}^{2}}{\eta_{x}^{2}}\Big)}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{34}}

\displaystyle\underline{+\frac{2(1-\alpha)}{\alpha^{3}}\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{3\alpha}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}^{3}+\frac{6}{\alpha^{2}}\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{\alpha}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}+\frac{2}{\alpha}\rho_{0}^{1+\frac{\alpha}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}\rho_{0}^{\alpha}}_{:=\mathcal{G}_{35}}.

For
\mathcal{G}_{28}
, since
\alpha>\frac{1}{3}
, one gets from Lemmas
6.11
,
7.3
-
7.4
,
7.7
-
7.8
and
A.4
-
A.5
, and Corollary
7.2
that

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{28}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{t}U_{xx}\big|_{2}+C(T)\left(\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\right)\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{tx}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\Big(\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\eta_{xx}\big|_{2}\left|\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta\big|_{2}\Big)\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}U_{t}\right|_{\infty}

(7.55)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\left\|\eta_{xx}\right\|_{1,1}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}\eta\big|_{2}\Big)\sum_{j=0}^{2}\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{3\alpha}{2}}\partial_{x}^{j}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}\eta\big|_{2}\big)\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big),

where one has used the fact that

(7.56)

\|\eta_{xx}\|_{1,1}\leq C\sum_{j=2}^{4}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{j}\eta\big|_{2}\leq C(T)+C\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}\eta\big|_{2}.

Similar to
\mathcal{G}_{28}
, for
\mathcal{G}_{29}
-
\mathcal{G}_{32}
, according to (
7.23
), (
7.56
), Lemmas
6.4
,
6.11
,
7.3
-
7.4
,
7.7
-
7.8
and
A.4
-
A.5
, Corollary
7.2
, the range of
\varepsilon_{0}
defined in (
1.18
), and
\alpha>\frac{1}{3}
, one gets that

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{29}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\left(\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\right|_{\infty}+\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\left|\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\right)\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{t}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{xx}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big(1+\left\|\eta_{xx}\right\|_{1,1}\big)\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big),

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{30}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\left\|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\right\|_{3}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{x}\big|_{\infty}+C(T)\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\left|\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{x}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta\big|_{2}\left|U_{x}\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\left|\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\eta_{xx}\big|_{2}\left|U_{x}\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\left\|\eta_{xx}\right\|_{1,1}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}\eta\big|_{2}\Big)

(7.57)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big),

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{31}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}\eta\big|_{2}\left|U_{x}\right|_{\infty}+C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta\big|_{2}\left|\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\left|U_{x}\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\eta_{xx}\big|_{2}\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\left|\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\left|U_{x}\right|_{\infty}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\left\|\eta_{xx}\right\|_{1,1}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}\eta\big|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big),

\displaystyle\left|\mathcal{G}_{32}\right|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\left(\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}\eta\right|_{\infty}+\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\right|_{\infty}\left|\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}+\left|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\left|\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\right)\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}U_{xx}\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\quad+C(T)\left|\eta_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\left\|\eta_{xx}\right\|_{1,1}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}\eta\big|_{2}\Big)

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big).

For
\mathcal{G}_{33}
-
\mathcal{G}_{35}
, via the similar arguments for dealing with
\mathcal{G}_{28}
-
\mathcal{G}_{32}
, one can obtain

(7.58)

\left|\mathcal{G}_{33}\right|_{2}+\left|\mathcal{G}_{34}\right|_{2}+\left|\mathcal{G}_{35}\right|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big).

It should be pointed out here that, when
\frac{1}{3}<\alpha<1
, the condition
\varepsilon_{0}<\frac{1}{\alpha}-1
has been used to ensure that

\rho_{0}^{1-\frac{3\alpha}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\alpha}\in L^{2}
in obtaining the
L^{2}
-norm of
\mathcal{G}_{35}
.

Thus, it follows from (
7.54
)-(
7.55
) and (
7.57
)-(
7.58
) that

\displaystyle\Big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U+\Big(\frac{1}{\alpha}+2\Big)\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\partial_{x}^{3}U\Big|_{2}\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big),

which, along with Proposition
C.1
and Lemma
7.7
, implies that

\displaystyle\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C\Big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U+\Big(\frac{1}{\alpha}+2\Big)\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{1}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{x}\partial_{x}^{3}U\Big|_{2}

(7.59)

\displaystyle\quad+C\left|(\rho_{0}^{\alpha})_{xx}\right|_{\infty}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{3}U\big|_{2}

\displaystyle\leq C(T)\Big(1+\int_{0}^{t}\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U\big|_{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\Big).

Finally, it follows from Grönwall’s inequality and (
7.59
) that

(7.60)

\big|\rho_{0}^{\left(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0}\right)\alpha}\partial_{x}^{4}U(t)\big|_{2}\leq C(T)\ \ \text{for all }0\leq t\leq T.

The proof of Lemma
7.9
is completed.
∎

8.
Global Existence and Uniqueness for the Nonlinear Problem

Based on the local existence and uniqueness results in Theorem
1.1
ii)
and Theorem
5.1
and the global estimates established in
§
6
-§
7
, we now give the proofs of
Theorems
1.2
and
1.4
.

8.1.
Proof of Theorem
1.2

Let
\overline{T}_{*}
be the maximal existence time of the unique local
classical solution
(U,\eta)
obtained in Theorem
1.1
ii).
Then
\overline{T}_{*}\geq T_{*}>0
. We claim that

\overline{T}_{*}=\infty.

Suppose, on the contrary, that
\overline{T}_{*}<\infty
. For every

0<T<\overline{T}_{*}
, we apply the estimates in
Lemmas
7.2
-
7.4
and

7.7
-
7.9
on
[0,T]
. Since

T<\overline{T}_{*}
, the constants in these estimates can be bounded
in terms of
\overline{T}_{*}
, and hence

(8.1)

\sup_{0\leq t<\overline{T}_{*}}\widetilde{E}(t,U)\leq C(\overline{T}_{*}).

Similarly, Lemmas
6.4
and
6.11
imply

(8.2)

C^{-1}(\overline{T}_{*})\leq\eta_{x}(t,x)\leq C(\overline{T}_{*})\quad\text{for all }(t,x)\in[0,\overline{T}_{*})\times\bar{I}.

Here and below,
C(\overline{T}_{*})
is a positive constant depending
only on
(\alpha,\varepsilon_{0},|I|,\mathcal{C}_{1},\mathcal{C}_{2},\rho_{0},u_{0},\overline{T}_{*})
and
C(\overline{T}_{*})
is increasing in
\overline{T}_{*}
and finite. Fix any
\tau\in(0,\overline{T}_{*})
and take

u_{*}(x)=U(\tau,x),\qquad\eta_{*}(x)=\eta(\tau,x).

It follows from (
8.1
)-(
8.2
) that

C^{-1}(\overline{T}_{*})\leq(\eta_{*})_{x}(x)\leq C(\overline{T}_{*})\quad\text{for all }x\in\bar{I}.

Thus, the constants
(c_{*},c^{*})
in Theorem
5.1

can be chosen independently of
\tau
.

We next verify the weighted regularity (
5.17
) of
\eta_{*}
. It follows from
(
1.12
) that

\partial_{x}^{k}\eta_{*}(x)=\delta_{1k}+\int_{0}^{\tau}\partial_{x}^{k}U(s,x)\,\mathrm{d}s,\qquad k=1,2,3,4.

Therefore, by (
8.1
), (
8.2
), and the Hölder inequality,

\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\eta_{*})_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{k=2}^{4}\big|\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k}\eta_{*}\big|_{2}^{2}\leq C(\overline{T}_{*})|\rho_{0}|_{1}+\tau\int_{0}^{\tau}\sum_{k=2}^{4}\big|\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k}U\big|_{2}^{2}\,\mathrm{d}s\leq C(\overline{T}_{*}).

Since
\rho_{0}
is fixed, (
8.1
)
and the above show that there exists a
constant
\mathfrak{M}>0
, independent of
\tau
, such that

(8.3)

\displaystyle 2+\|\rho_{0}^{\alpha}\|_{3}+\big|\rho_{0}^{\frac{1}{2}}(\eta_{*})_{x}\big|_{2}^{2}+\sum_{k=2}^{4}\big|\rho_{0}^{(\frac{3}{2}-\varepsilon_{0})\alpha}\partial_{x}^{k}\eta_{*}\big|_{2}^{2}+\widetilde{E}(\tau,U)\leq\mathfrak{M}.

The temporal derivatives
(U_{t},U_{tx},U_{txx},U_{tt})
at
t=\tau
entering

\widetilde{E}(\tau,U)
are the actual temporal derivatives of the
existing classical solution. Since
(U,\eta)
satisfies
equations
\eqref{secondreformulation}_{1}
-
\eqref{secondreformulation}_{2}
,
(U_{t},U_{tx},U_{txx},U_{tt})|_{t=\tau}
satisfy (
5.21
). Consequently, all the hypotheses of
Theorem
5.1
are satisfied by
(\rho_{0},u_{*},\eta_{*})
with
constants independent of
\tau
; in particular, the compatibility
relations at
t=\tau
follow from those of the existing classical
solution.

Theorem
5.1
thus provides a time
T_{0}>0
, depending
only on
(c_{*},c^{*},\mathcal{C}_{1},\mathcal{C}_{2},\alpha,\varepsilon_{0},|I|,\mathfrak{M})
and independent of
\tau
, such that the problem with the initial data
(U,\eta)\big|_{t=\tau}=(u_{*},\eta_{*})
admits a unique classical solution
(U^{\sharp},\eta^{\sharp})
on
[\tau,\tau+T_{0}]\times\bar{I}
.

We now choose
\tau<\overline{T}_{*}
sufficiently close to
\overline{T}_{*}
so that
\overline{T}_{*}-\tau<T_{0}
. The solution
(U^{\sharp},\eta^{\sharp})
and the original solution
(U,\eta)
solve the same system with the same reference density
\rho_{0}
and the same initial data at
t=\tau
. For every
S\in(\tau,\overline{T}_{*})
, both solutions are defined on
[\tau,S]\times\bar{I}
and belong to the solution class specified in Theorem
5.1
. The uniqueness stated in Theorem
5.1
implies
(U^{\sharp},\eta^{\sharp})=(U,\eta)
on
[\tau,S]\times\bar{I}
. Since
S<\overline{T}_{*}
is arbitrary,
(U^{\sharp},\eta^{\sharp})
thus extends
(U,\eta)
to
[0,\tau+T_{0}]\times\bar{I}
. However,
\tau+T_{0}>\overline{T}_{*}
, which contradicts the maximality of
\overline{T}_{*}
. Therefore,
\overline{T}_{*}=\infty
.
